# Every expression in the utility files Derive shipped, and in their demo
# files, extracted from the archives under `artifacts/sessions/`.
#
# One expression per line, in file order. `;` comments are already stripped and
# `~` continuation lines already joined, so each line is one whole expression.
# Each `##` group is replayed with a fresh symbol table, so the declarations an
# earlier line of a group makes are in force for the later ones.
#
# Two kinds of line are left out. Expressions that are nothing but a string
# literal, which Derive's files use as commentary, carry nothing the shorter
# conformance corpus does not already cover. And a handful of expressions that
# only parse in a session this replay cannot reproduce:
#
#   FIT_.MTH     `c_:=[c0_,...]` and `c__:=[c0__,...]` need Word mode, since
#                Character mode reads `c0_` as `c`, `0`, `_`.
#   TENSOR1.DMO  `lhs-rhs` is `LHS(-RHS(...))` unless CaseMode is Sensitive,
#                and LHS then has no operand.
#   TENSOR2.DMO  three lines pass `x` to a function, but an earlier `X(r):=`
#                in that file made `x` an arbitrary function, which needs an
#                operand of its own.

## derive-1.62-dos/ALGEBRA.MTH
(x+a)^2-2*a*x
2 (x^2 - y^2)^6 - (x^2 - y^2)^5(2 x^2 - 3)
(x + (a + 1)^10) ^ 2  -  (a + 1)^20
a^3/((a-b)*(a-c)) + b^3/((b-c)*(b-a)) + c^3/((c-a)*(c-b))
(x^6+a^6)(x+1)/((x^6+a^6)*(x^2-a^2)+a^2*x^2*(x^4-a^4))+a^2*x^2(x+1)/(x^6-a^6-a^2*x^2*(x^2-a^2))
((a*n + b*m)^2 + (a*m - b*n)^2) / ((a*p + b*Q)^2 + (a*Q - b*p)^2)
((p*x^2+(k-s)*x+r)^2-(p*x^2+(k+s)*x+r)^2)/((p*x^2+(k+t)*x+r)^2-(p*x^2+(k-t)*x+r)^2)
(1 - (1-(y+1)/(x+y+1)) / (1-x/(x+y+1))) / ((y+1)^2 - x / (1+x/(y-x+1)) (x (y+1)/(y-x+1) - x))
((1/x^2+1/y^2)/(1/x^2-1/y^2)-(1/x^2-1/y^2)/(1/x^2+1/y^2))/((2x+y)/(((x+y)/(x-y)+(x-y)/(x+y))*(x^2/y^2+y^2/x^2-2)))

## derive-1.62-dos/APPROX.MTH
CF(a,m,n):=VECTOR(VECTOR(ELEMENT(a,m-n+i+j),j,1,n+1),i,1,n)
DV(a,m,n):=ELEMENT(ROW_REDUCE(CF(a,m,n))`,n+1)
NV(a,u,m,n):=VECTOR(ELEMENT(a,k+1)-SUM(ELEMENT(u,n+1-i)*ELEMENT(a,k+1-i),i,1,k),k,1,MIN(m,n))
P3(a,v,u,x,c,m,n):=(ELEMENT(a,1)+SUM(ELEMENT(v,k)*(x-c)^k,k,1,m))/(1-SUM(ELEMENT(u,n+1-k)*(x-c)^k,k,1,n))
P2(a,u,x,c,m,n):=P3(a,NV(a,u,m,n),u,x,c,m,n)
P1(a,x,c,m,n):=P2(a,DV(a,m,n),x,c,m,n)
PADE(wx,x,c,m,n):=P1(VECTOR(LIM(DIF(wx,x,k),x,c)/k!,k,0,m+n+1),x,c,m,n)
PADE(EXP(x),x,0,2,2)

## derive-1.62-dos/COORD.MTH
CYLINDRICAL:=[[r, theta, z], [1, r, 1]]
SPHERICAL:=[[r, theta, phi], [1, r*SIN(phi), r]]
JACOBIAN(u,v):=VECTOR(VECTOR(DIF(ELEMENT(u,i),ELEMENT(v,j)),j,DIMENSION(v)),i,DIMENSION(u))
COVARIANT_METRIC_TENSOR(u):=u`.u
SQRT_DIAGONAL(u):=VECTOR(SQRT(ELEMENT(ELEMENT(u,j),j)),j,DIMENSION(u))

## derive-1.62-dos/DERIV.MTH
LINE(x,y,m,t):=y+m*(t-x)
TANGENT_LINE(u,x,a):=LINE(a,LIM(u,x,a),LIM(DIF(u,x),x,a),x)
PERPENDICULAR(u,x,a):=LINE(a,LIM(u,x,a),(-1)/LIM(DIF(u,x),x,a),x)
CURVATURE(u,x,a):=LIM(DIF(u,x,2)/(1+DIF(u,x)^2)^(3/2),x,a)
CIRCLE(x,y,r,t):=[x+r*COS(t), y+r*SIN(t)]
OSCULATING_CIRCLE(u,x,a):=CIRCLE(a-LIM(DIF(u,x),x,a)/(CURVATURE(u,x,a)*SQRT(1+LIM(DIF(u,x),x,a)^2)),LIM(u,x,a)+1/(CURVATURE(u,x,a)*SQRT(1+LIM(DIF(u,x),x,a)^2)),1/CURVATURE(u,x,a),x)
IMPLICIT_DIF(u,y,x):=(-DIF(u,x)/DIF(u,y))

## derive-1.62-dos/INTEGRAL.MTH
ARC_LENGTH(u,x,a,b):=INT(SQRT(1+DIF(u,x)^2),x,a,b)
VECTOR_ARC_LENGTH(v,t,a,b):=INT(SQRT(SUM(DIF(ELEMENT(v,k_),t)^2,k_,1,DIMENSION(v))),t,a,b)
VOLUME_OF_REVOLUTION(u,x,a,b):=PI*INT(u^2,x,a,b)
SURFACE_OF_REVOLUTION(u,x,a,b):=2*PI*INT(u*SQRT(1+DIF(u,x)^2),x,a,b)
FOURIER(u,x,a,b,n):=1/(b-a)*INT(u,x,a,b)+2/(b-a)*SUM(COS(2*PI*k_*x/(b-a))*INT(u*COS(2*PI*k_*x/(b-a)),x,a,b)+SIN(2*PI*k_*x/(b-a))*INT(u*SIN(2*PI*k_*x/(b-a)),x,a,b),k_,1,n)
LAPLACE(u,t,s):=INT(#e^(-s*t)*u,t,0,INF)

## derive-1.62-dos/ODE1.MTH
SEPARABLE(px,qy,x,y_,x0,y0):=INT(1/qy,y_,y0,y_)=INT(px,x,x0,x)
EXACT_IF_0(pxy,qxy,x,y_):=DIF(pxy,y_)-DIF(qxy,x)
EXACT(pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=INT(pxy,x,x0,x)+INT(LIM(qxy,x,x0),y_,y0,y_)=0
USE_INTEG_FCTR(mu,pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=EXACT(mu*pxy,mu*qxy,x,y_,x0,y0)
LINEAR1(px,qx,x,y_,x0,y0):=USE_INTEG_FCTR(#e^INT(px,x),px*y_-qx,1,x,y_,x0,y0)
BERNOULLI(px,qx,k,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(LINEAR1((1-k)*px,(1-k)*qx,x,y_,x0,y0),y_,y_^(1-k))
HOMOGENEOUS_IF_FREE_OF_X(rxy,x,y_):=LIM(rxy,y_,x*y_)
HOMOGENEOUS(rxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(SEPARABLE(1/x,LIM(rxy,x,1)-y_,x,y_,x0,y0/x0),y_,y_/x)
FUN_LIN_CCF(qxy,a,b,c,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(SEPARABLE(1,a+b*LIM(qxy,y_,(y_-a*x-c)/b),x,y_,x0,a*x0+b*y0+c),y_,a*x+b*y_+c)
LIN_FRAC_AUX(rxy,h_,k_,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(LIM(HOMOGENEOUS(LIM(LIM(rxy,y_,y_+k_),x,x+h_),x,y_,x0-h_,y0-k_),y_,y_-k_),x,x-h_)
LIN_FRAC(rxy,a,b,c,s_,t_,d_,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIN_FRAC_AUX(rxy,(b*d_-c*t_)/(a*t_-b*s_),(c*s_-a*d_)/(a*t_-b*s_),x,y_,x0,y0)
TEST_FREE_OF_X(pxy,qxy,x,y_):=(DIF(qxy,x)-DIF(pxy,y_))/pxy
INTEG_FCTR_FREE_OF_X(test_result,pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=USE_INTEG_FCTR(#e^INT(test_result,y_),pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0)
TEST_FREE_OF_Y(pxy,qxy,x,y_):=(DIF(pxy,y_)-DIF(qxy,x))/qxy
INTEG_FCTR_FREE_OF_Y(test_result,pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=USE_INTEG_FCTR(#e^INT(test_result,x),pxy,qxy,x,y_,x0,y0)
TESTM2(v_,x,y_):=x^ELEMENT(v_,1)*y_^ELEMENT(v_,2)
TESTM1(v_,x,y_):=TESTM2(ELEMENT(ROW_REDUCE([LIM(LIM(v_,y_,5),x,2), LIM(LIM(v_,y_,3),x,7)])`,3),x,y_)
TEST_MONOMIAL(pxy,qxy,x,y_):=TESTM1([y_*qxy, -x*pxy, x*y_*(DIF(pxy,y_)-DIF(qxy,x))],x,y_)
GEN_HOM_IF_CONST(rxy,x,y_):=x*DIF(x*rxy/y_,x)/(y_*DIF(x*rxy/y_,y_))
GEN_HOM(rxy,k_,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(SEPARABLE(1/x,y_*(k_+LIM(rxy*x/y_,y_,y_/x^k_)),x,y_,x0,y0*x0^k_),y_,y_*x^k_)
ALMOST_LIN_IF_FREE_OF_X(rxy,hy,y_):=DIF(hy,y_)/rxy
ALMOST_LIN(rxy,hy,px,qx,x,y_,x0,y0):=LIM(LINEAR1(px*DIF(hy,y_)/rxy,qx*DIF(hy,y_),x,y_,x0,LIM(hy,y_,y0)),y_,hy)
CLAIRAUT(fxvy,gv,x,y_,v_):=[LIM(fxvy=gv,v_,@1), x*LIM(DIF(LIM(fxvy,y_,x*v_-y_),y_),y_,x*v_-y_)=DIF(gv,v_)]
SOLVABLE_Y(rxv,x,v_):=(v_-DIF(rxv,x))/DIF(rxv,v_)
SOLVABLE_X(ryv,y_,v_):=(1-v_*DIF(ryv,y_))/(v_*DIF(ryv,v_))
LIM_2(uxy,x,x0,y_,y0):=LIM(LIM(uxy,y_,y0),x,x0)
TAY3(r1,r3,x,y_,x0,y0):=(x-x0)^3*LIM_2(r3,x,x0,y_,y0)/6+(x-x0)^4*LIM_2(DIF(r3,x)+r1*DIF(r3,y_),x,x0,y_,y0)/4!
TAY2(r1,r2,x,y_,x0,y0):=(x-x0)^2*LIM_2(r2,x,x0,y_,y0)/2+TAY3(r1,DIF(r2,x)+r1*DIF(r2,y_),x,y_,x0,y0)
TAY_ODE1(rxy,x,y_,x0,y0):=y0+(x-x0)*LIM_2(rxy,x,x0,y_,y0)+TAY2(rxy,DIF(rxy,x)+rxy*DIF(rxy,y_),x,y_,x0,y0)
PICARD(rxy,yprev,x,y_,x0,y0):=y0+INT(LIM(rxy,y_,yprev),x,x0,x)

## derive-1.62-dos/ODE2.MTH
LIN2_RED_CCF_DISC(p_,q_):=p_^2-4*q_
LIN2_RED_CCF_POS(p_,q_,x):=@1*#e^((SQRT(p_^2-4*q_)-p_)*x/2)+@2*#e^((-p_-SQRT(p_^2-4*q_))*x/2)
LIN2_RED_CCF_NEG(p_,q_,x):=#e^(-p_*x/2)*(@1*SIN(x*SQRT(4*q_-p_^2)/2)+@2*COS(x*SQRT(4*q_-p_^2)/2))
LIN2_RED_CCF_0(p_,x):=#e^(-p_*x/2)*(@1+@2*x)
WRONSKIAN(ux,vx,x):=DET([[ux, vx], [DIF(ux,x), DIF(vx,x)]])
LIN2_COMPLETE(ux,vx,rx,x):=vx*INT(ux*rx/WRONSKIAN(ux,vx,x),x)-ux*INT(vx*rx/WRONSKIAN(ux,vx,x),x)
SECOND_RED_PARTIC(px,ux,x):=ux*INT(#e^INT(px,x)/ux^2,x)
AUTONOMOUS_CONSERVATIVE(qy,x,y_,x0,y0,v0):=x=x0+INT((2*INT(qy,y_,y0,y_)+v0^2)^(-1/2),y_,y0,y_)
AUTONOMOUS(rvy,v_):=rvy/v_
IMPOSE_BV2(x,y_,x1,y1,x2,y2):=SOLVE([LIM(y_,x,x1)=y1, LIM(y_,x,x2)=y2],[@1, @2])
IMPOSE_IC2(x,y_,x0,y0,v0):=SOLVE([LIM(y_,x,x0)=y0, LIM(DIF(y_,x),x,x0)=v0],[@1, @2])
LIOUVILLE(px,qy,x,y_):=INT(#e^INT(qy,y_),y_)=@1+@2*INT(#e^INT(-px,x),x)
EXACT2_IF_ZEROS(f_,g_,x,y_,v_):=[(v_*DIF(f_,y_,2)+2*DIF(DIF(f_,x),y_)-DIF(DIF(g_,y_),v_))*v_+DIF(f_,x,2)-DIF(DIF(g_,x),v_)+DIF(g_,y_), v_*DIF(DIF(f_,y_),v_)+DIF(DIF(f_,x),v_)+2*DIF(f_,y_)-DIF(g_,v_,2)]
EXACT2_AUX3(s_,h_,x):=s_+INT(h_-DIF(s_,x),x)
EXACT2_AUX2(r_,x,y_,v_):=EXACT2_AUX3(INT(DIF(r_,v_),y_),LIM(r_,v_,0),x)
EXACT2_AUX1(p_,g_,x,y_,v_):=p_+EXACT2_AUX2(g_-DIF(p_,x)-v_*DIF(p_,y_),x,y_,v_)
EXACT2(f_,g_,x,y_,v_):=EXACT2_AUX1(INT(f_,v_),g_,x,y_,v_)=@1
EULER(p_,q_,b_):=[p/b-1, q/b^2]
EQUIDIMENSIONAL_IF_FREE_OF_Y(rxyv,y_,v_):=LIM(rxyv,v_,y_*v_)/y_
EQUIDIMENSIONAL_Y(rxyv,y_,v_):=LIM(rxyv,y_,1)-v_^2
EQUIDIMENSIONAL_IF_FREE_OF_X(rxyv,x,v_):=x^2*LIM(rxyv,v_,v_/x)
EQUIDIMENSIONAL_X(rxyv,x,v_):=(v_+LIM(rxyv,x,1))/v_
HOMOGENEOUS2_IF_FREE_OF_X(rxyv,x,y_):=x*LIM(rxyv,y_,x*y_)
HOMOGENEOUS2(rxyv,x,y_,v_):=(LIM(LIM(rxyv,x,1),v_,v_+y_)-v_)/v_
HOMOGENEOUS2((y-x*v)^2/(n*x^3),x,y,v)

POLAR_CENTER_OF_CURVATURE(r,theta):=PARA_CENTER_OF_CURVATURE([r*COS(theta),r*SIN(theta)],theta)
POLAR_TANGENT(r,theta,theta0,x):=PARA_TANGENT([r*COS(theta),r*SIN(theta)],theta,theta0,x)
POLAR_PERPENDICULAR(r,theta,theta0,x):=PARA_PERPENDICULAR([r*COS(theta),r*SIN(theta)],theta,theta0,x)
POLAR_OSCULATING_CIRCLE(r,theta,theta0,phi):=PARA_OSCULATING_CIRCLE([r*COS(theta),r*SIN(theta)],theta,theta0,phi)
IMP_DIF_AUX(d1,x,y,n):=ITERATE(DIF(dk,x)+d1*DIF(dk,y),dk,d1,n-1)
[default1:=1,IMP_DIF(u,x,y,default1):=IMP_DIF_AUX(-DIF(u,x)/DIF(u,y),x,y,default1)]
IMP_CURVATURE(u,x,y):=CURVATURE_AUX(IMP_DIF(u,x,y,1),IMP_DIF(u,x,y,2))
IMP_CENTER_OF_CURVATURE(u,x,y):=CENTER_OF_CURVATURE_AUX([x,y],IMP_DIF(u,x,y,1),IMP_DIF(u,x,y,2))
IMP_TANGENT(u,x,y,x0,y0):=LIM(LINE(x_,y_,LIM(IMP_DIF(u,x,y,1),[x,y],[x_,y_]),x),[x_,y_],[x0,y0])
IMP_PERPENDICULAR(u,x,y,x0,y0):=LIM(LINE(x_,y_,-1/LIM(IMP_DIF(u,x,y,1),[x,y],[x_,y_]),x),[x_,y_],[x0,y0])
IMP_OSCULATING_CIRCLE(u,x,y,x0,y0,theta):=CIRCLE(LIM(IMP_CENTER_OF_CURVATURE(u,x,y),[x,y],[x0,y0]),1/LIM(IMP_CURVATURE(u,x,y),[x,y],[x0,y0]),x,theta)
TANGENT_PLANE(u,v,v0):=LIM(GRAD(u,v),v,v0) . (v-v0)
NORMAL_LINE(u,v,v0,t):=v0+t*LIM(GRAD(u,v),v,v0)

## derive-3.14-dos/HYPERGEO.MTH

AREA(x,x1,x2,y,y1,y2,default1):=INT(INT(default1,y,y1,y2),x,x1,x2)

VOLUME(x,x1,x2,y,y1,y2,z,z1,z2,default1):=INT(INT(INT(default1,z,z1,z2),y,y1,y2),x,x1,x2)

VOLUME_INERTIA(x,x1,x2,y,y1,y2,z,z1,z2,default1):=VOLUME(x,x1,x2,y,y1,y2,z,z1,z2,[[y^2+z^2,-x*y,-x*z],[-x*y,x^2+z^2,-y*z],[-x*z,-y*z,x^2+y^2]]*default1)/VOLUME(x,x1,x2,y,y1,y2,z,z1,z2,default1)
SPHERICAL_VOLUME(r,r1,r2,theta,theta1,theta2,phi,phi1,phi2,default1):=INT(INT(INT(default1*r^2*SIN(theta),phi,phi1,phi2),theta,theta1,theta2),r,r1,r2)
CYLINDRICAL_VOLUME(r,r1,r2,theta,theta1,theta2,z,z1,z2,default1):=INT(INT(INT(default1*r,r,r1,r2),theta,theta1,theta2),z,z1,z2)
VOLUME_OF_REVOLUTION(y,x,x1,x2):=CYLINDRICAL_VOLUME(r_,0,y,theta_,0,2*pi,x,x1,x2)
AREA_OF_REVOLUTION(y,x,x1,x2):=2*pi*PARA_ARC_LENGTH([x,y],x,x1,x2,y)
VOLUMEY_OF_REVOLUTION(y,x,x1,x2):=2*pi*INT(x*y,x,x1,x2)

default1:=1

DISTINCT_PARTS_AUX(n,m):=IF(n<2*m,1,1+SUM(DISTINCT_PARTS_AUX(n-k_,k_+1),k_,m,FLOOR(n,2)))
DISTINCT_PARTS(n):=IF(n<1,0,DISTINCT_PARTS_AUX(n-1,1))
PARTS(n):=IF(n<2,1,FLOOR(APPROX(SUM(1/pi*SQRT(k_/2)*SUM(IF(GCD(h_,k_)=1,COS(pi*(SUM((i_/k_-1/2)*(MOD(i_*h_/k_)-1/2),i_,1,k_-1)-2*n*h_/k_)),0),h_,1,k_)*(-2*SQRT(6)*#e^(-pi*SQRT(24*n-1)/(6*k_))*(#e^(pi*SQRT(24*n-1)/(3*k_))*(6*k_-pi*SQRT(24*n-1))-6*k_-pi*SQRT(24*n-1))/(k_*(24*n-1)^(3/2))),k_,1,SQRT(n)/LOG(n,11)),LOG(1/(4*n*SQRT(3))*EXP(pi*SQRT(2*n/3)),10)+5)+0.5))
DISTINCT_PARTS(n):=SUM(IF(8*n-16*i_+1=FLOOR(SQRT(8*n-16*i_+1))^2,PARTS(i_),0),i_,0,n/2)

## derive-3.14-dos/NUMERIC.MTH
DIF_NUM(y,x,x0,h):=(LIM(y,x,x0+h)-LIM(y,x,x0-h))/(2*h)
DIF2_NUM(y,x,x0,h):=(LIM(y,x,x0+h)-2*LIM(y,x,x0)+LIM(y,x,x0-h))/h^2
SMOOTH_VECTOR(v):=VECTOR(IF(m_=1 OR m_=DIMENSION(v),v SUB m_,(v SUB (m_-1)+v SUB m_+v SUB (m_+1))/3),m_,DIMENSION(v))

SMOOTH_COLUMN(a,j):=SMOOTH_COLUMN_AUX(a`,j)`
DIF_DATA_AUX(v1,v2):=(v1 SUB 1^2*v2 SUB 2-v1 SUB 2*v2 SUB 1^2)/(v1 SUB 1*v2 SUB 1*(v1 SUB 1-v2 SUB 1))
DIF_DATA(a):=VECTOR([a SUB m_ SUB 1,IF(m_=1,DIF_DATA_AUX(a SUB 2-a SUB 1,a SUB 3-a SUB 1),IF(m_=DIMENSION(a),DIF_DATA_AUX(a SUB (m_-1)-a SUB m_,a SUB (m_-2)-a SUB m_),DIF_DATA_AUX(a SUB (m_+1)-a SUB m_,a SUB (m_-1)-a SUB m_)))],m_,DIMENSION(a))

## derive-3.14-dos/ODE1.MTH
LINEAR1(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=y=(y0+INT(q*#e^INT(p,x,x0,x),x,x0,x))/#e^INT(p,x,x0,x)
LINEAR1_GEN(p,q,x,y,c):=y=(c+INT(q*#e^INT(p,x),x))/#e^INT(p,x)
SEPARABLE(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=INT(1/q,y,y0,y)=INT(p,x,x0,x)
SEPARABLE_GEN(p,q,x,y,c):=INT(1/q,y)=INT(p,x)+c
HOMOGENEOUS(r,x,y,x0,y0):=IF(DIF(LIM(r,y,x*y),x),LIM(SEPARABLE(1/x,LIM(r,x,1)-y,x,y,x0,y0/x0),y,y/x),"inapplicable","inapplicable")
HOMOGENEOUS_GEN(r,x,y,c):=IF(DIF(LIM(r,y,x*y),x),LIM(SEPARABLE_GEN(1/x,LIM(r,x,1)-y,x,y,c),y,y/x),"inapplicable","inapplicable")

EXACT_GEN_AUX(s,q,y):=s+INT(q-DIF(s,y),y)
EXACT_GEN(p,q,x,y,c):=IF(DIF(q,x)=DIF(p,y),EXACT_GEN_AUX(INT(p,x),q,y)=c,"inapplicable","inapplicable")

INTF_GEN_HLP(p,q,x,y,c):= IF(DIF((DIF(p,y)-DIF(q,x))/q,y),    INTF_GEN_AUX(#e^INT((DIF(p,y)-DIF(q,x))/q,x),p,q,x,y)=c,    "inapplicable",    "inapplicable")
INTEGRATING_FACTOR_GEN(p,q,x,y,c):= IF(DIF((DIF(q,x)-DIF(p,y))/p,x),    INTF_GEN_AUX(#e^INT((DIF(q,x)-DIF(p,y))/p,y),p,q,x,y)=c,    INTF_GEN_HLP(p,q,x,y,c),    INTF_GEN_HLP(p,q,x,y,c))
FUN_LIN_CCF(r,p,q,k,x,y,x0,y0):=LIM(SEPARABLE(1,p+q*LIM(r,y,(y-p*x-k)/q),x,y,x0,p*x0+q*y0+k),y,p*x+q*y+k)

LIN_FRAC_AUX(r,p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=LIM(HOMOGENEOUS(LIM(r,[y,x],[y+q,x+p]),x,y,x0-p,y0-q),[y,x],[y-q,x-p])

GEN_HOM_AUX(k,r,x,y,x0,y0):=IF(DIF(k,x)=0 AND DIF(k,y)=0,LIM(SEPARABLE(1/x,y*(k+LIM(r*x/y,y,y/x^k)),x,y,x0,y0*x0^k),y,y*x^k),"inapplicable","inapplicable")

CLAIRAUT(p,q,x,y,v,c):=[LIM(p-q,v,c)=0,x*LIM(DIF(LIM(p,y,x*v-y),y),y,x*v-y)-DIF(q,v)=0]

SEP(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):= IF(DIF(p,y)=0 and DIF(q,x)=0,    SEPARABLE(p,-1/q,x,y,x0,y0),    SEP_HLP(p,q,x,y,x0,y0),    SEP_HLP(p,q,x,y,x0,y0))
DSOLVE1(p,q,x,y,x0,y0,a_):= IF("inapplicable"=a_:=INTEGRATING_FACTOR(p,q,x,y,x0,y0),    IF("inapplicable"=a_:=HOMOGENEOUS(-p/q,x,y,x0,y0),       IF("inapplicable"=a_:=SEP(p,q,x,y,x0,y0),	  GEN_HOM(-p/q,x,y,x0,y0),a_,a_),a_,a_),a_,a_)

TAYLOR_ODE1(r,x,y,x0,y0,n,v_):=ELEMENT(ITERATE([v_ SUB 1+1,DIF(v_ SUB 2,x)+r*DIF(v_ SUB 2,y),v_ SUB 3+(x-x0)^v_ SUB 1*LIM(v_ SUB 2,[x,y],[x0,y0])/v_ SUB 1!],v_,[1,r,y0],n),3)

CHANGE_X_1(w,x,y,y1,y2,p,q,r,k,i,u):=LIM(ODE_2ND_1(LIM(k*y1-(p*y2+q*y1-r*y2-w)/r,x,i),x,y,y1,y2),x,u)

CHANGE_X_3(w,x,y,y1,y2,p,q,r,k,i,u):=IF(  FREE(k,x) AND 	FREE(	    local:==ODE_2ND_1(			     LIM(k*y1-(p*y2+q*y1-r*y2-w)/r,x,INVERT_(u,x)),			     x,			     y,			     y1,			     y2			     ),	    fail	    ),  LIM(local,x,u),  CHANGE_X_2(w,x,y,y1,y2,p,q,r,k,i,u)  )

SYMMETRIC_TEST_1(v):=IF(v=[] OR (v=[a] OR (v=[b] OR (v=[a,b] OR v=[b,a]))),true,false,false)

SYMMETRIC_G_1(w,x,y,y1,y2):=ODE_2ND_1(	 BARE(TRANSFORM_Y(TRANSFORM_X(w,#e^x,x,y,y1,y2),#e^y,x,y,y1,y2),y2),	 x,	 y,	 y1,	 y2	 )
SYMMETRIC_G(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  SYMMETRIC_TEST([x,y,y1,y2],[a*x,b*y,b*y1/a,b*y2/a^2],w),  LIM(SYMMETRIC_G_1(LIM(w,[x,y],[t,u]),t,u,y1,y2),[t,u],[LN(x),LN(y)]),  fail  )
SYMMETRIC_N_1(n,w,x,y,y1,y2):=ODE_2ND_1(	 BARE(	     TRANSFORM_Y(			TRANSFORM_X(w,#e^x,x,y,y1,y2),			#e^(n*x)*y,			x,			y,			y1,			y2			),	     y2	     ),	 x,	 y,	 y1,	 y2	 )
SYMMETRIC_N_2(n,w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  FREE(n,x) AND FREE(n,y) AND FREE(n,y1) AND FREE(n,y2),  IF(    SYMMETRIC_TEST([x,y,y1,y2],[a*x,a^n*y,a^(n-1)*y1,a^(n-2)*y2],w),    LIM(SYMMETRIC_N_1(n,LIM(w,[x,y],[t,u]),t,u,y1,y2),[t,u],[LN(x),y/x^n]),    fail    ),  fail  )
SYMMETRIC_N_3(n,w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(n=0,fail,SYMMETRIC_N_2(n,w,x,y,y1,y2),SYMMETRIC_N_2(n,w,x,y,y1,y2))
SYMMETRIC_N_4(g,w,x,y,y1,y2):=SYMMETRIC_N_3(([0,y1,2*y2] . g-x*DIF(w,x))/([y,y1,y2] . g),w,x,y,y1,y2)

SYMMETRIC_N(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  fail=(local:==SYMMETRIC_N_5(BARE(w,y2),x,y,y1,y2)),  SYMMETRIC_N_5(w,x,y,y1,y2),  local,  local  )
SYMMETRIC_Y(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  SYMMETRIC_TEST([y,y1,y2],a*[y,y1,y2],w),  LIM(     ODE_2ND_1(BARE(TRANSFORM_Y(LIM(w,y,t),#e^t,x,t,y1,y2),y2),x,t,y1,y2),     t,     LN(y)     ),  fail  )
SYMMETRIC_X(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  SYMMETRIC_TEST([x,y1,y2],[a*x,y1/a,y2/a^2],w),  LIM(     ODE_2ND_1(BARE(TRANSFORM_X(LIM(w,x,t),#e^t,t,y,y1,y2),y2),t,y,y1,y2),     t,     LN(x)     ),  fail  )
TRIVIAL_2ND_1(q,w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  w*GRAD(DIF(w,y2),[x,y,y1])=DIF(w,y2)*GRAD(w,[x,y,y1]),  APPEND(	IF(	  FREE(w,y1),	  [w*q=0],	  IF(	    fail=(local:==ODE_1ST(w*q,alpha,x,y,y1)),	    [w*q=0],	    IF(NUMBER(DIMENSION(local)),local,[local]),	    IF(NUMBER(DIMENSION(local)),local,[local])	    )	  ),	[TRIVIAL_SET(w,y,SOLVE(w,y2),x^2/2,c1_*x+c2_,y2)=0]	),  fail,  fail  )
TRIVIAL_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2):=FIX_DIM(TRIVIAL_2ND_1(1/BARE(w,y2),w,x,y,y1,y2))
CANONIC_FACTOR(p,q,x):=#e^INT(q/(2*p),x)
CANONIC_TERM(p,q,r,x):=(2*q*DIF(p,x)-2*p*DIF(q,x)+4*r*p-q^2)/(4*p^2)
POLES_1(v,u,d,x):=VECTOR(-1/DIF(LN(v SUB (u SUB (d-e__))),x),e__,0,d-1)
POLES_2(v,u,x):=POLES_1(v,u,DIMENSION(u),x)
POLES_3(v,x):=POLES_2(v,SELECT(LIM(v SUB f__,x,inf)=0,f__,1,DIMENSION(v)),x)
POLES_4(t,x):=POLES_3(FACTORS(FACTOR(t,Trivial,x)),x)
POLES_5(v):=VECTOR(RHS(i),i,v)
POLES_6(u,x):=POLES_5(       IF(	 DIMENSION(u)/=1,	 ITERATE(		APPEND(SOLVE(u SUB j,x),SOLVE(u SUB DIMENSION(u),x)),		j,		1,		DIMENSION(u)-1		),	 SOLVE(u SUB 1,x),	 SOLVE(u SUB 1,x)	 )       )
POLES_7(u,x):=IF(DIMENSION(SELECT(x=g__,g__,VARIABLES(u)))=1,POLES_6(u,x),[],[])
POLES_8(t,x):=LEAN(POLES_7(POLES_4(t,x),x))
POLES_9(t,v):=IF(  DIMENSION(v)=0,  [0,0],  IF(    DIMENSION(v)=1,    [v SUB 1,0],    v,    v    ),  fail  )
POLES(t,x):=POLES_9(t,POLES_8(t,x))
LINEAR_1(t,f,x,r):=IF(  t<0,  [f*#e^SQRT(-t),f*#e^(-SQRT(-t)),fail],  IF(    t=0,    [f,f*(x-r),fail],    [f*COS(SQRT(t)),f*SIN(SQRT(t)),fail],    [f*COS(SQRT(t)),f*SIN(SQRT(t)),fail]    ),  [f*#e^SQRT(-t),f*#e^(-SQRT(-t)),fail]  )
LINEAR(t,f,x,r):=LINEAR_1((x-r)^2*t,IF(FREE(t,x),1,x-r)/f,x,r)
EULER_1(t,f,x,r,a,b):=(x-r)/(f*SQRT(b))*IF(  1>a,  [b^(SQRT(1-a)/2),b^(-SQRT(1-a)/2),fail*SQRT(b)*f/(x-r)],  IF(    1=a,    [1,LN(b),fail*SQRT(b)*f/(x-r)],    [COS(SQRT(a-1)/2*LN(b)),SIN(SQRT(a-1)/2*LN(b)),fail*SQRT(b)*f/(x-r)],    [COS(SQRT(a-1)/2*LN(b)),SIN(SQRT(a-1)/2*LN(b)),fail*SQRT(b)*f/(x-r)]    ),  [b^(SQRT(1-a)/2),b^(-SQRT(1-a)/2),fail*SQRT(b)*f/(x-r)]  )
EULER(t,f,x,r,s):=IF(  r*s=0,  EULER_1(t,f,x,r,4*t*(x-r)^2,x-r),  EULER_1(t,f,x,r,4*t*(x-r)^2*(x-s)^2/(r-s)^2,(x-r)/(x-s)),  EULER_1(t,f,x,r,4*t*(x-r)^2*(x-s)^2/(r-s)^2,(x-r)/(x-s))  )
BESSEL_1(f,v,z,k,x,r):=BES_SOL(v/k,z*(x-r)^k/k,SQRT(x-r)/f,x)
BESSEL_2(t,f,z,k,x,r):=BESSEL_1(f,SQRT(4*z^2+1-LIM(t,x,1))/2,z,k,x,r)
BESSEL(t,f,k,x,r):=BESSEL_2(	t,	f,	SQRT(	    LIM((SELECT(NOT(FREE(h__,x)),h__,TERMS(EXPAND(t,x)))) SUB 1,x,1)	    )/2,	k,	x,	r	)
KUMMER_1(f,a,b,z,k,x,r):=F11_SOL(a/k,(b+k-1)/k,z*(x-r)^k/k,(x-r)^(b/2)*#e^(-z*(x-r)^k/(2*k))/f,x)
KUMMER_2(t,f,b,z,k,x,r):=KUMMER_1(f,(b+k-1)/2-z/2-LIM(x*DIF(t,x),x,1)/(4*k*z),b,z,k,x,r)
KUMMER(t,f,k,x,r):=KUMMER_2(	t,	f,	SQRT(1-IF(SIGNUM(k)=1,LIM(t,x,0),LIM(t,x,inf),LIM(t,x,0)))+1,	SQRT(	     -IF(		SIGNUM(k)=1,		LIM(t*x^(-2*k),x,inf),		LIM(t*x^(-2*k),x,0),		LIM(t*x^(-2*k),x,0)		)	    ),	k,	x,	r	)
GAUSS_1(f,a,b,c,z,k,x,r):=F21_SOL(       (a+b)/(2*k),       (b-a)/(2*k),       (c+k-1)/k,       z*(x-r)^k,       (x-r)^(c/2)*(z*(x-r)^k-1)^((b-c+1)/(2*k))/f,       x       )
GAUSS_2(f,q,z,k,x,r):=GAUSS_1(       f,       SQRT(z^2-q SUB 1)/z,       (SQRT(k^2*z^2-z^2*q SUB 3-z*q SUB 2-q SUB 1)+		z*(SQRT(1-q SUB 3)+k))/z,       SQRT(1-q SUB 3)+1,       z,       k,       x,       r       )
GAUSS_3(f,g,h,i,z,k,x,r):=GAUSS_2(f,[-(g*k-h*k-i)/k,(2*g*k-2*h*k-i)/k,h],z,k,x,r)
GAUSS_4(u,f,z,k,x,r):=GAUSS_3(f,LIM(u,x,1),LIM(u,x,0),LIM(DIF(u,x),x,1),z,k,x,r)
GAUSS_5(v,f,z,k,x,r):=GAUSS_4(v*(z*x^k-1)^2,f,z,k,x,r)
GAUSS(v,f,k,x,r,s):=GAUSS_5(4*v*x^2,f,(s-r)^(-k),k,x,r)
L_TYPE(t,f,x,r):=IF(FREE(t,x) OR FREE(t*(x-r)^4,x),LINEAR(t,f,x,r),fail)
E_TYPE(t,f,x,r,s):=IF(  FREE(t*(x-r)^2,x) OR FREE(t*(x-r)^2*(x-s)^2,x),  EULER(t,f,x,r,s),  fail  )
C_TYPE_1(a,b,t,f,x):=IF(  FREE(b,x) AND NOT(FREE(a,x)),  KUMMER(4*x^2*LIM(t,x,2*x-a/b),f,2,x,x-a/b),  fail  )
C_TYPE(t,f,x):=C_TYPE_1(DIF(t,x),DIF(t,x,2),t,f,x)
B_TYPE(v,x):=IF(DIMENSION(v)=1 AND FREE(x*DIF(LN(v SUB 1),x),x),true,false,false)
K_TYPE(v,x):=IF(  DIMENSION(v)=2 AND (FREE(x*DIF(LN(v SUB 1),x),x) AND 	(FREE(x*DIF(LN(v SUB 2),x),x) AND (FREE(v SUB 1/v SUB 2^2,x) OR 	FREE(v SUB 2/v SUB 1^2,x)))),  true,  false,  false  )
G_TYPE_1(u,g,f,x,r):=IF(  K_TYPE(u SUB 2,x),  KUMMER(	4*x^2*g,	f,	x*(DIF(LN(u SUB 2 SUB 1),x)+DIF(LN(u SUB 2 SUB 2),x))/3,	x,	r	),  fail,  fail  )
G_TYPE(u,g,f,x,r):=IF(  u SUB 1=[],  IF(    B_TYPE(u SUB 2,x),    BESSEL(	  4*x^2*g,	  f,	  x/(2*DIMENSION(u SUB 2))*DIF(LN(u SUB 2 SUB 1),x),	  x,	  r	  ),    G_TYPE_1(u,g,f,x,r),    G_TYPE_1(u,g,f,x,r)    ),  fail,  fail  )
H_TYPE(u,g,f,x,r,s):=IF(  (K_TYPE(u SUB 1,x) AND K_TYPE(u SUB 2,x) OR B_TYPE(u SUB 1,x) AND 	B_TYPE(u SUB 2,x)) AND FREE(u SUB 2 SUB 1/u SUB 1 SUB 1,x),  GAUSS(g,f,x/DIMENSION(u SUB 1)*DIF(LN(u SUB 1 SUB 1),x),x,r,s),  fail,  fail  )
CANONIC_1(u,g,t,f,x,r,s):=IF(fail=(local:==L_TYPE(t,f,x,r)),IF(fail=(local:==E_TYPE(t,f,x,r,s)),IF(fail=(local:==C_TYPE(t,f,x)),IF(fail=(local:==G_TYPE(u,g,f,x,r)),H_TYPE(u,g,f,x,r,s),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local)
CANONIC_2(q,g,t,f,x,r,s):=CANONIC_1([X_TERMS(q SUB 1,x),X_TERMS(q SUB 2,x)],g,t,f,x,r,s)
CANONIC_3(g,t,f,x,r,s):=CANONIC_2(PARTS(x^2*g),g,t,f,x,r,s)
CANONIC_4(t,f,x,r,s):=CANONIC_3(LIM(t,x,x+r),t,f,x,r,s)
CANONIC_5(t,f,x,u):=IF(  1/u SUB 1=0 OR 1/u SUB 2=0,  fail,  CANONIC_4(t,f,x,u SUB 1,u SUB 2),  CANONIC_4(t,f,x,u SUB 1,u SUB 2)  )
CANONIC_6(t,f,x):=CANONIC_5(t,f,x,POLES(t,x))
CANONIC_7(w,x,y,y1,y2):=CANONIC_6(	 CANONIC_TERM(DIF(w,y2),DIF(w,y1),DIF(w,y),x),	 CANONIC_FACTOR(DIF(w,y2),DIF(w,y1),x),	 x	 )
CANONIC_8(g,m,n,q,x):=c1_*BARE(m,x)+c2_*BARE(n,x)-m*INT(n*g/q,x)+n*INT(m*g/q,x)
CANONIC_9(g,m,n,o,x):=CANONIC_8(g,m,n,WRONSKY(m,n,x,o),x)
CANONIC_A(w,l,u,x,y,y2):=IF(  fail=u,  fail,  y=CANONIC_9((l-w)/DIF(w,y2),u SUB 1,u SUB 2,u SUB 3,x),  y=CANONIC_9((l-w)/DIF(w,y2),u SUB 1,u SUB 2,u SUB 3,x)  )
CANONIC_B(w,l,x,y,y1,y2):=CANONIC_A(w,l,CANONIC_7(l,x,y,y1,y2),x,y,y2)
CANONIC(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  GRAD(GRAD(w,[y,y1,y2]),[y,y1,y2]),  CANONIC_B(w,y2*DIF(w,y2)+y1*DIF(w,y1)+y*DIF(w,y),x,y,y1,y2),  fail,  fail  )
ODE_2ND_1(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_Y_P(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_Y(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==CANONIC(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_X_P(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_X(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==LIOUVILLE(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==EXACT_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2)),SPECIAL_A(w,x,y,y1,y2),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local)
ODE_2ND_2(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(fail=(local:==ODE_2ND_1(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==EXACT_LINEAR(w,x,y,y1,y2)),CHANGE_X(w,x,y,y1,y2),local,local),local,local)
ODE_2ND_3(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(fail=(local:==DERIVED(w,x,y,y1,y2,y3)),IF(fail=(local:==SYMMETRIC_G(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==SYMMETRIC_Y(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==SYMMETRIC_X(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_Y_P(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_Y(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_X_P(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==MISSING_X(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==EXACT_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==LIOUVILLE(w,x,y,y1,y2)),SYMMETRIC_N(w,x,y,y1,y2),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local),local,local)
ODE_2ND_4(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(  GRAD(GRAD(w,[y,y1,y2]),[y,y1,y2]),  ODE_2ND_2(w,x,y,y1,y2),  ODE_2ND_3(w,x,y,y1,y2),  ODE_2ND_3(w,x,y,y1,y2)  )
ODE_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2):=IF(fail=(local:==TRIVIAL_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2)),IF(fail=(local:==FACTORED(w,x,y,y1,y2)),ODE_2ND_4(w,x,y,y1,y2),local,local),local,local)
ODE_1(w,x,y,y1,y2,a):=IF(  FREE(w,y1) AND FREE(w,y2),  MESSAGE(6),  IF(    FREE(w,y2),    ODE_1ST(w,a,x,y,y1),    ODE_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2),    ODE_2ND(w,x,y,y1,y2)    )  )
ODE(w,x,y,y1,y2,alpha):=ODE_1(SIDE(w),x,y,y1,y2,alpha)

## derive-3.14-dos/USERS/PSERIES.MTH
InputMode:=Word
CaseMode:=Sensitive
EM1:="Denom.series must have non-zero constant term!"
EM2:="Second arg.must have zero constant term!"
EM3:="Series must start with linear term!"
rPle(e,v,n):=VECTOR(IF(iI=n,e,ELEMENT(v,iI)),iI,DIMENSION(v))
dGt(T,x):=IF(T=0,0,0,x*DIF(T,x)/T)
dGp(P,x,n):=IF(P=0,IF(n<0,0,n-1),dGp(DIF(P,x),x,n+1),dGp(DIF(P,x),x,n+1))
DEGP(P,x):=dGp(P,x,0)
nZe(A,B,zt,n):=IF(n<1,B,nZe(A,IF(zt=1,APPEND([ELEMENT(A,n)],B),IF(ELEMENT(A,n)=0,B,APPEND([ELEMENT(A,n)],B),APPEND([ELEMENT(A,n)],B))),IF(zt=0,0,IF(ELEMENT(A,n)=0,1,0,0)),n-1))
CONTR_S(A):=nZe(A,[],IF(ELEMENT(A,DIMENSION(A))=0,1,0,0),DIMENSION(A))
pSx(P,x,v,n,N):=IF(n>N AND P=0,v,pSx(DIF(P,x),x,APPEND(v,[LIM(P,x)*x^n/n!]),n+1,N),pSx(DIF(P,x),x,APPEND(v,[LIM(P,x)*x^n/n!]),n+1,N))
SERIES(P,x,N):=CONTR_S(pSx(P,x,[],0,IF(N>0,N,0,0)))
POLY(S):=IF(DIMENSION(S)=0,0,SUM(ELEMENT(S,iI),iI,DIMENSION(S)),S)
TAYLOR_S(FCT,x,x0,N):=SERIES(TAYLOR(FCT,x,x0,N),x,N)
eXts(S,x,E,d,k,n):=IF(k>DIMENSION(S),E,IF(n<d+1,eXts(S,x,APPEND(E,[0]),d,k,n+1),eXts(S,x,APPEND(E,[ELEMENT(S,k)]),dGt(ELEMENT(S,k+1),x),k+1,n+1),eXts(S,x,APPEND(E,[0]),dGt(ELEMENT(S,k+1),x),k+1,n+1)))
ALLP_S(S,x):=eXts(S,x,[],dGt(ELEMENT(S,1),x),1,1)
lTs(A,n):=IF(ELEMENT(A,n)=0,lTs(A,n+1),ELEMENT(A,n),ELEMENT(A,n))
lTi(A,n):=IF(n>DIMENSION(A),n,IF(ELEMENT(A,n)=0,lTi(A,n+1),n,n))
tRpr(AP1,AP2,N):=VECTOR(SUM(ELEMENT(AP1,iI)*ELEMENT(AP2,nN+1-iI),iI,1,nN),nN,N)
sUms(A1,A2,x,N):=VECTOR(ELEMENT(A1,iI)+ELEMENT(A2,iI),iI,N)
SUM_S(S1,S2,x):=CONTR_S(sUms(ALLP_S(S1,x),ALLP_S(S2,x),x,MIN(DIMENSION(ALLP_S(S1,x)),DIMENSION(ALLP_S(S2,x)))))
pRs(A1,A2,x):=tRpr(A1,A2,MIN(lTi(A1,1)+DIMENSION(A2),lTi(A2,1)+DIMENSION(A1))-1)
PROD_S(S1,S2,x):=CONTR_S(pRs(ALLP_S(S1,x),ALLP_S(S2,x),x))
qTs(A,B,C,b0,N,n):=IF(n>N,C,qTs(A,B,APPEND(C,[(ELEMENT(A,n)-SUM(ELEMENT(C,iI)*ELEMENT(B,n+1-iI),iI,n-1))/b0]),b0,N,n+1))
QUOT_S(S1,S2,x):=IF(dGt(lTs(S2,1),x)=0,CONTR_S(qTs(ALLP_S(S1,x),ALLP_S(S2,x),[],ELEMENT(S2,1),MIN(DIMENSION(ALLP_S(S1,x)),DIMENSION(ALLP_S(S2,x))),1)),EM1,EM1)
dGs(A,x,d,n):=IF(ELEMENT(A,n)=0,dGs(A,x,d+1,n-1),dGt(ELEMENT(A,n),x)+d,dGt(ELEMENT(A,n),x)+d)
RECIP_S(S,x):=QUOT_S(APPEND([1],VECTOR(0,iI,dGs(S,x,0,DIMENSION(S)))),S,x)
fFctr(A,B,x,Bn,T,N,n):=IF(n>N,T,fFctr(A,B,x,tRpr(Bn,B,N),T+ELEMENT(A,n)*Bn/x^(n-1),N,n+1))
fFct(A,B,x,N):=fFctr(A,B,x,tRpr(B,B,N),rPle(ELEMENT(A,1),ELEMENT(A,2)*VECTOR(ELEMENT(B,jJ),jJ,N)/x,1),N,3)
FFCT_S(A,B,x):=IF(dGt(lTs(B,1),x)>0,CONTR_S(fFct(ALLP_S(A,x),ALLP_S(B,x),x,MIN(DIMENSION(ALLP_S(A,x)),DIMENSION(ALLP_S(B,x))))),EM2,EM2)
iNvr(A,x,An,T,C,N,n):=IF(n>N,C,iNvr(A,x,tRpr(An,A,N),T-An*ELEMENT(T,n)/ELEMENT(A,2)^(n-1),APPEND(C,[-ELEMENT(T,n)/ELEMENT(A,2)^(n-1)*x^(n-1)]),N,n+1))
iNva(A,x,N):=iNvr(A,x,tRpr(A,A,N),x/ELEMENT(A,2)*A,[0,x^2/ELEMENT(A,2)],N,3)
INV_S(S,x):=IF(dGt(lTs(S,1),x)=1,CONTR_S(iNva(ALLP_S(S,x),x,DIMENSION(ALLP_S(S,x)))),EM3,EM3)
hGs(a,b,z,T,S,k,K):=IF(k=K,APPEND(S,[T]),hGs(a,b,z,z*T*PRODUCT(ELEMENT(a,iI)+k,iI,DIMENSION(a))/((k+1)*PRODUCT(ELEMENT(b,jJ)+k,jJ,DIMENSION(b))),APPEND(S,[T]),k+1,K))
GHYP_S(U,L,z,K):=hGs(U,L,z,z*PRODUCT(ELEMENT(U,iI),iI,DIMENSION(U))/PRODUCT(ELEMENT(L,jJ),jJ,DIMENSION(L)),[1],1,K)

TR(a,b,n):=ITERATES(NEW_ROW(v,a),v,[1,b-a,(b-a)*(F(a)+F(b))/2],n)
R1(m_,p,i,j,n):=IF(j<n,m_ SUB i SUB j,IF(i<n-2,"***",(2^p*m_ SUB i SUB (j-1)-m_ SUB (i-1) SUB (j-1))/(2^p-1)))

RAT2(m_,i,j,n):=IF(j<3,m_ SUB i SUB j,IF(i=1 OR (i<j-1 OR i=n),"***",IF(m_ SUB i SUB j=m_ SUB (i+1) SUB j,"***",RAT1(m_,i,j))))

## derive-3.14-dos/USERS/SPLINE.MTH
Precision:=Approximate
PrecisionDigits:=10
NotationDigits:=6
TR1(m_):=[m_ SUB 1 SUB 2,m_ SUB 1 SUB 3,m_ SUB 1 SUB 4,2]
TR2(m_,v_,k):=[m_ SUB k SUB 2-m_ SUB k SUB 1/v_ SUB 3*v_ SUB 1,m_ SUB k SUB 3,m_ SUB k SUB 4-m_ SUB k SUB 1/v_ SUB 3*m_ SUB (k-1) SUB 3,k+1]

TR3(m_,n):=[m_ SUB n SUB 1/m_ SUB n SUB 3,n-1]
TR4(m_,v_,k):=[(m_ SUB k SUB 1-m_ SUB k SUB 2*v_ SUB 1)/m_ SUB k SUB 3,k-1]
BACK_SUB(m_):=ITERATES(TR4(m_,v_,v_ SUB 2),v_,TR3(m_,DIMENSION(m_)),DIMENSION(m_)-1)
REVERSE(m_):=VECTOR(m_ SUB k SUB 1,k,DIMENSION(m_),1,-1)
SOLVE_TRI(m_):=REVERSE(BACK_SUB(R_REDUCE(m_)))
SP1(m_):=VECTOR(m_ SUB (k+1)-m_ SUB k,k,1,DIMENSION(m_)-1)
SP2(mm):=VECTOR([mm SUB k SUB 1,mm SUB k SUB 2/mm SUB k SUB 1],k,1,DIMENSION(mm))
SP3(mm,k):=[mm SUB k SUB 1,mm SUB (k+1) SUB 2-mm SUB k SUB 2,mm SUB (k+1) SUB 1,2*(mm SUB k SUB 1+mm SUB (k+1) SUB 1)]
first_row:=[0,0,0,1]
last_row:=[0,0,0,1]
SP4(mm):=APPEND([first_row],VECTOR(SP3(mm,k),k,1,DIMENSION(mm)-1),[last_row])
SP5(m_):=APPEND(m_`,[SOLVE_TRI(SP4(SP2(SP1(m_))))])`
SP6(x,y,w,h,p,q,t):=[x+h*t,h^2*t*(q*(t^2-1)+3*w*(t-1))+p*t+y]
SP7(m,k):=APPEND(m SUB k,m SUB (k+1)-m SUB k)
SP8(v,t):=SP6(v SUB 1,v SUB 2,v SUB 3,v SUB 4,v SUB 5,v SUB 6,t)
SPLINE1(m_,t):=VECTOR(SP8(SP7(m_,k),t),k,1,DIMENSION(m_)-1)
SPLINE(m,t):=SPLINE1(SP5(m),t)
F(x):=
DIFFERENCE(cc):=cc-VECTOR([0,F(cc SUB k SUB 1)],k,1,DIMENSION(cc))
PARA1(s1,s2):=VECTOR([s1 SUB k SUB 2,s2 SUB k SUB 2],k,1,DIMENSION(s1))
PARA2(m1,m2,t):=PARA1(SPLINE(m1,t),SPLINE(m2,t))
PARA(m,t):=PARA2(VECTOR([k,m SUB k SUB 1],k,1,DIMENSION(m)),VECTOR([k,m SUB k SUB 2],k,1,DIMENSION(m)),t)

NZEL_T(A):=nZel(lInarx(A,RANK_T(A)),RANK_T(A),DIMENSION(A))
iNze(A1):=(ITERATE(IF(A1 SUB v2=0,[v1,v2+1],[APPEND(v1,[v2]),v2+1],[APPEND(v1,[v2]),v2+1]),[v1,v2],[[],1],DIMENSION(A1))) SUB 1
xNze1(A1,iNz,kEy,r,d):=ITERATE(IF(A1 SUB (iNz SUB v1)=kEy,[APPEND(v3,[iVl(iNz SUB v1,r,d)]),v2,v1+1],IF(A1 SUB (iNz SUB v1)=-kEy,[APPEND(v3,-[iVl(iNz SUB v1,r,d)]),v2,v1+1],[v3,APPEND(v2,[iNz SUB v1]),v1+1]),IF(A1 SUB (iNz SUB v1)=-kEy,[APPEND(v3,-[iVl(iNz SUB v1,r,d)]),v2,v1+1],[v3,APPEND(v2,[iNz SUB v1]),v1+1],[v3,APPEND(v2,[iNz SUB v1]),v1+1])),[v3,v2,v1],[[],[],1],DIMENSION(iNz))
xNze2(C,Z,kEy):=[APPEND(C SUB 1,[[Z SUB 1=kEy]]),Z SUB 2]
xNze(A1,C,r,d):=xNze2(C,xNze1(A1,C SUB 2,A1 SUB (C SUB 2 SUB 1),r,d),A1 SUB (C SUB 2 SUB 1))
gNze1(A1,C,r,d):=IF(DIMENSION(C SUB 2)=0,C SUB 1,gNze1(A1,xNze(A1,C,r,d),r,d))
gNze(A1,r,d):=gNze1(A1,[[],iNze(A1)],r,d)
GNZE_T(A):=gNze(lInarx(A,RANK_T(A)),RANK_T(A),DIMENSION(A))
oDaux(A,ivA,x,d):=vNestx(DIF(eLt(A,ivA,1),x SUB dD_),APPEND(ivA,[dD_]),d,DIMENSION(ivA)+1)
OD_T(A,x):=LIM(oDaux(A,iVa_(RANK_T(A)),x,dIm),dIm,DIMENSION(x))
cDaux(A,ncov,weight,x,C_ij_k,rA,iv,ivR,dim):=OD_T(A,x)-SUM(IP_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,iI_),C_ij_k,[iv SUB iI_,kK,sS],ivR),iI_,1,ncov)+SUM(IP_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,jJ_),C_ij_k,[sS,kK,iv SUB jJ_],ivR),jJ_,ncov+1,rA)-IF(weight=0,0,weight*OP_T(A,iv,CT_T(C_ij_k,[iI,sS,sS],[iI]),[kK],ivR))
CD_T(A,ncov,weight,x,C_ij_k):=cDaux(A,ncov,weight,x,C_ij_k,RANK_T(A),iVa_(RANK_T(A)),APPEND(iVa_(RANK_T(A)),[kK]),DIMENSION(x))
lDaux(A,v,ncov,weight,x,odv,rA,iv):=IP_T(OD_T(A,x),APPEND(iv,[sS]),v,[sS],iv)+SUM(IP_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,iI_),odv,[sS,iv SUB iI_],iv),iI_,1,ncov)-SUM(IP_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,jJ_),odv,[iv SUB jJ_,sS],iv),jJ_,ncov+1,rA)+IF(weight=0,0,weight*TRACE(odv)*A)
LD_T(A,v,ncov,weight,x):=lDaux(A,v,ncov,weight,x,OD_T(v,x),RANK_T(A),iVa_(RANK_T(A)))
dIvt(A,iv_zZ,x):=SUM(DIF(eLt(A,iv_zZ,1),x SUB zZ_),zZ_,DIMENSION(x))
dVrgx(A,ncov,weight,sA,x,C_ij_k,iv,ivD,C_i):=vNestx(dIvt(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(zZ_,iv,sA),x),ivD,DIMENSION(x),DIMENSION(ivD))-IF(ncov<1,0,SUM(CI_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(tT,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,iI_),sA),C_ij_k,[iv SUB iI_,tT,sS],ivD),iI_,1,ncov))+IF(ncov<0,0,SUM(IF(jJ_=sA,0,CI_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(tT,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,jJ_),sA),C_ij_k,[sS,tT,iv SUB jJ_],ivD)),jJ_,ncov+1,DIMENSION(iv)))+IF(weight=1,0,(1-weight)*IP_T(A,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,iv,sA),C_i,[sS],ivD))
DV_T(A,ncov,weight,sA,x,C_ij_k):=dVrgx(A,ncov,weight,sA,x,C_ij_k,iVa_(RANK_T(A)),DELETE_ELEMENT(iVa_(RANK_T(A)),sA),CT_T(C_ij_k,[iI,sS,sS],[iI]))
eQel(v,i,n):=IF(i>0,n,IF(n<2,0,eQel(v,lOel(v,v SUB n,n-1),n-1)))
sGnp(v):=SIGN(PRODUCT(PRODUCT(v SUB jJ-v SUB iI,jJ,iI+1,DIMENSION(v)),iI,1,DIMENSION(v)-1))
dTgc(iv,A,x):=sGnp(iv)*DIF(eLt(A,DELETE_ELEMENT(iv,DIMENSION(iv)),1),x SUB (iv SUB DIMENSION(iv)))
eLgc(Miv,A,x):=sGnp(Miv SUB 1)*SUM(dTgc(Miv SUB sSp,A,x),sSp,DIMENSION(Miv))
gCaux(A,x,ivD,r):=LIM(vNestx(IF(eQel(ivD,0,r)>0,0,eLgc(pErme(ivD,[[]],1),A,x)),ivD,dIm,r),dIm,DIMENSION(x))
GC_T(A,x):=gCaux(A,x,iVa_(RANK_T(A)+1),RANK_T(A)+1)
JACOB(x,x_):=VECTOR(VECTOR(DIF(x SUB iI,x_ SUB jJ),jJ,DIMENSION(x_)),iI,DIMENSION(x))
tFidx(A,JC,ivA,ivc,ic):=vNestx(SUM(JC SUB sS SUB ic*eLt(A,ivc,1),sS,DIMENSION(A)),ivA,DIMENSION(A),DIMENSION(ivA))
tFaux(A,ivA,ncov,JM,JMc,n):=IF(n>DIMENSION(ivA),A,tFaux(tFidx(A,IF(n>ncov,JMc,JM),ivA,REPLACE_ELEMENT(sS,ivA,n),ivA SUB n),ivA,ncov,JM,JMc,n+1))
TF_T(A,ncov,weight,JM,JM_):=tFaux(A,iVa_(RANK_T(A)),ncov,JM,JM_`,1)*IF(weight=0,1,DET(JM)^weight)
CHRIS1(g_ij,x):=1/2*VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(DIF(g_ij SUB jJ SUB kK,x SUB iI)+DIF(g_ij SUB kK SUB iI,x SUB jJ)-DIF(g_ij SUB iI SUB jJ,x SUB kK),kK,1,DIMENSION(g_ij)),jJ,1,DIMENSION(g_ij)),iI,1,DIMENSION(g_ij))
CHRIS2(g_ij,g__ij,x):=VECTOR(1/2*(VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(DIF(g_ij SUB jJ SUB kK,x SUB iI)+DIF(g_ij SUB kK SUB iI,x SUB jJ)-DIF(g_ij SUB iI SUB jJ,x SUB kK),kK,1,DIMENSION(g_ij)),jJ,1,DIMENSION(g_ij)),iI,1,DIMENSION(g_ij))) SUB lL . g__ij,lL,1,DIMENSION(g_ij))
EL_RIEM1(C_ijk,C_ij_k,x,i,j,k,l):=IF(i=j OR k=l,0,DIF(C_ijk SUB j SUB l SUB i,x SUB k)-DIF(C_ijk SUB j SUB k SUB i,x SUB l)+C_ijk SUB i SUB l . C_ij_k SUB j SUB k-C_ijk SUB i SUB k . C_ij_k SUB j SUB l)
RIEM1(C_ijk,C_ij_k,x):=VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(EL_RIEM1(C_ijk,C_ij_k,x,iI,jJ,kK,lL),lL,DIMENSION(C_ijk)),kK,DIMENSION(C_ijk)),jJ,DIMENSION(C_ijk)),iI,DIMENSION(C_ijk))
vT3i1(A1,d,i2,i3):=VECTOR(A1 SUB iI_,iI_,d*(i2-1)+i3,d^3,d^2)
EL_RIEM2(C_ij_k,x,i,j,k,l):=IF(k=l,0,DIF(C_ij_k SUB j SUB l SUB i,x SUB k)-DIF(C_ij_k SUB j SUB k SUB i,x SUB l)+vT3i1(APPEND(APPEND(C_ij_k)),DIMENSION(x),k,i) . C_ij_k SUB j SUB l-vT3i1(APPEND(APPEND(C_ij_k)),DIMENSION(x),l,i) . C_ij_k SUB j SUB k)
RIEM2(C_ij_k,x):=VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(IF(kK=lL,0,DIF(C_ij_k SUB jJ SUB lL SUB iI,x SUB kK)-DIF(C_ij_k SUB jJ SUB kK SUB iI,x SUB lL)+vT3i1(APPEND(APPEND(C_ij_k)),DIMENSION(x),kK,iI) . C_ij_k SUB jJ SUB lL-vT3i1(APPEND(APPEND(C_ij_k)),DIMENSION(x),lL,iI) . C_ij_k SUB jJ SUB kK),lL,DIMENSION(x)),kK,DIMENSION(x)),jJ,DIMENSION(x)),iI,DIMENSION(x))
vT3i2(A1,d,i1,i3):=VECTOR(A1 SUB iI_,iI_,(i1-1)*d^2+i3,i1*d^2+i3-d,d)
rIcx(C_ij_k,x,T):=VECTOR(VECTOR(DIV(C_ij_k SUB iI SUB jJ,x)-DIF(T SUB iI,x SUB jJ)-SUM(vT3i2(APPEND(APPEND(C_ij_k)),DIMENSION(x),iI,sS) . C_ij_k SUB sS SUB jJ,sS,DIMENSION(x))+C_ij_k SUB iI SUB jJ . T,jJ,DIMENSION(x)),iI,DIMENSION(x))
RICCI(C_ij_k,x):=rIcx(C_ij_k,x,VECTOR(TRACE(C_ij_k SUB kK),kK,DIMENSION(x)))
eInstx(R_ij,g_ij,RH):=R_ij-RH*g_ij
EINST(R_ij,g_ij,g__ij):=eInstx(R_ij,g_ij,TRACE(g__ij . R_ij)/2)
wEylx(R_ijkl,R_ij,g_ij,R,d):=VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(VECTOR(eLt(R_ijkl,[iI,jJ,kK,lL],1)+(g_ij SUB iI SUB lL*R_ij SUB kK SUB jJ+g_ij SUB jJ SUB kK*R_ij SUB lL SUB iI-g_ij SUB iI SUB kK*R_ij SUB lL SUB jJ-g_ij SUB jJ SUB lL*R_ij SUB kK SUB iI+(g_ij SUB iI SUB kK*g_ij SUB lL SUB jJ-g_ij SUB iI SUB lL*g_ij SUB kK SUB jJ)*R/(d-1))/(d-2),lL,d),kK,d),jJ,d),iI,d)
WEYL(R_ijkl,R_ij,g_ij):=wEylx(R_ijkl,R_ij,g_ij,TRACE(g_ij^(-1) . R_ij),DIMENSION(g_ij))
dEcel(v):=VECTOR(v SUB iI-SIGN(v SUB iI),iI,DIMENSION(v))
DI_NZEL(A):=VECTOR([dEcel(LHS(A SUB kK SUB 1))=RHS(A SUB kK SUB 1)],kK,DIMENSION(A))
DI_GNZE(A):=VECTOR([VECTOR(dEcel((LHS(A SUB kK SUB 1)) SUB jJ),jJ,DIMENSION(LHS(A SUB kK SUB 1)))=RHS(A SUB kK SUB 1)],kK,DIMENSION(A))

## derive-3.14-dos/USERS/TENSOR1.DMO
E__ijk:==EPS(3)
RANK_T(E__ijk)
DIM_T(E__ijk)
NZEL_T(E__ijk)
GNZE_T(E__ijk)
T_ij:=[[A,B,C],[D,E,F],[G,H,I]]
T__ij_k:==IP_T(E__ijk,[iI,jJ,sS],T_ij,[kK,sS],[iI,jJ,kK])
S__i:==CT_T(T__ij_k,[iI,sS,sS],[iI])
S__i:==CI_T(E__ijk,[iI,sS,tT],T_ij,[sS,tT],[iI])
x_:=[r,theta,phi]

JM:==JACOB(x,x_)
JM_:==JM^-1
g_ij:==DELTA(3)
g_ij_:==TF_T(g_ij,2,0,JM,JM_)
g_ij_:==JM` . JM
g__ij_:==g_ij_^-1

C_ij_k_:==CHRIS2(g_ij_,g__ij_,x_)

[a:=,b:=,c:=,X:=ELEMENT(x,1)]
V__i:=[a*X,b,c]

V_i_:==TF_T(V__i,1,0,JM,JM_)
V_i_ . V__i_-V__i . V__i
DV_ij_:==CD_T(V_i_,1,0,x_,C_ij_k_)
DV_ij:==TF_T(DV_ij_,2,0,JM_,JM)

rhs:==C_ijk_+C_jki_
g_ijk_:==OD_T(g_ij_,x_)
lhs:==TP_T(g_ijk_,[iI,kK,jJ],[iI,jJ,kK])

## derive-3.14-dos/USERS/TENSOR2.DMO
x:=[r,theta,phi,t]

[X(r):=,Y(r):=]

## derive-3.14-dos/VECTOR.MTH
i_:=[1,0,0]
j_:=[0,1,0]
k_:=[0,0,1]
OUTER(v,w):=VECTOR([v SUB n_],n_,DIMENSION(v)) . [w]
KRONECKER(i,j):=IF(i=j,1,0)
ADJOIN_ELEMENT(e,v):=APPEND([e],v)
APPEND_COLUMNS(a,b):=APPEND(a`,b`)`
PARTITION(v,n,d):=IF(NUMBER(d),VECTOR(VECTOR(v SUB m_,m_,n_,n_+n-1),n_,1,1+d*FLOOR(DIMENSION(v)-n,d),d),PARTITION(v,n,n))
MINOR(a,i,j):=DELETE_ELEMENT(DELETE_ELEMENT(a,i)`,j)`
SWAP_ELEMENTS(v,i,j):=VECTOR(IF(m_=i,v SUB j,IF(m_=j,v SUB i,v SUB m_)),m_,DIMENSION(v))
SCALE_ELEMENT(v,i,s):=VECTOR(IF(m_=i,s*v SUB i,v SUB m_),m_,DIMENSION(v))
default1:=1
SUBTRACT_ELEMENTS(v,i,j,default1):=VECTOR(IF(m_=i,v SUB i-default1*v SUB j,ELEMENT(v,m_)),m_,DIMENSION(v))
FORCE0(a,i,j,p):=VECTOR(IF(m_=i,a SUB i-a SUB i SUB j/a SUB p SUB j*a SUB p,a SUB m_),m_,DIMENSION(a))
PIVOT(a,i,j):=VECTOR(IF(m_<=i,a SUB m_,a SUB m_-a SUB m_ SUB j/a SUB i SUB j*a SUB i),m_,DIMENSION(a))
MATPROD(a,b,i,j):=SUM(a SUB i SUB n_*b SUB n_ SUB j,n_,1,DIMENSION(b))
COFACTOR(a,i,j):=COS(pi*(i+j))*DET(MINOR(a,i,j))
ADJOINT(a):=VECTOR(VECTOR(COFACTOR(a,m_,n_),m_,DIMENSION(a)),n_,DIMENSION(a))
ZERO_VECTOR(v,i):=IF(i>DIMENSION(v),0,IF(v SUB i=0,ZERO_VECTOR(v,i+1),1,1))
NONZERO_ROWS(m):=SUM(ZERO_VECTOR(m SUB n_,1),n_,1,DIMENSION(m))
RANK(m):=NONZERO_ROWS(ROW_REDUCE(m))
v_:=[x1,x2,x3,x4]
EXACT_EIGENVECTOR(a,mu):=SOLVE(VECTOR(SUM((a SUB m_ SUB n_-mu*KRONECKER(m_,n_))*[x1,x2,x3,x4] SUB n_,n_,DIMENSION(a)),m_,DIMENSION(a)),VECTOR([x1,x2,x3,x4] SUB n_,n_,DIMENSION(a)))
APPROX_EIGENVECTOR(a,mu):=SIGN(ROW_REDUCE(a-mu*IDENTITY_MATRIX(DIMENSION(a)),VECTOR(3000*COS(pi*m_)/m_,m_,DIMENSION(a)))` SUB (DIMENSION(a)+1))
cylindrical:=[[r,theta,z],[1,r,1]]
spherical:=[[r,theta,phi],[1,r*SIN(phi),r]]
JACOBIAN(u,alpha):=VECTOR(GRAD(u SUB m_,alpha),m_,DIMENSION(u))
COVARIANT_METRIC_TENSOR(a):=a` . a
GEOMETRY_MATRIX(alpha,g):=[alpha,VECTOR(SQRT(g SUB m_ SUB m_),m_,DIMENSION(g))]

## derive-3.14-dos/ZETA.MTH
HURWITZ_ZETA(s,a,m):=(m+a+1/2)^(1-s)/(s-1)+SUM((k+a)^(-s),k,0,m)
LERCH_PHI(z,s,a,m):=SUM(z^k/(a+k)^s,k,0,m)
POLYLOG(n,z,m):=SUM(z^k/k^n,k,1,m)
DILOG(x):=INT(LN(t)/(1-t),t,1,x)

## hawaii-coursework/add-head.mth
[SUBST(u,x,a):=,SECANT(u,x,a,h):=,TANGENT(u,x,a):=,CURVEFIT(x,data):=,SPLINE(x,data,m1):=,NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=,DRAW_NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=,DRAW_COMPLEX(v):=,BISECT(u,x,v0,k):=,LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=,MID(u,x,n,a,b):=,RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=,TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=,SIMP(u,x,n,a,b):=,DRAW_LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=,DRAW_RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=,DRAW_TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=,DE(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=,DF(r,x,x0,xm,m,y,y0,yn,n):=,EULER(r,x,y,x0,y0,xn,n):=]

## hawaii-coursework/add-util-ver4.mth
SUBST(u,x,a):=LIM(u,x,a)
SECANT(u,x,a,h):=y=(SUBST(u,x,a+h)-SUBST(u,x,a))/h*(x-a)+SUBST(u,x,a)
TANGENT(u,x,a):=y=SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(DIF(u,x),x,a)*(x-a)
POLY(x,a,n):=SUM(a SUB (i+1)*x^i,i,0,n)
DPOLY(x,a,n):=SUM(i*a SUB (i+1)*x^(i-1),i,1,n)
EQNS(data,ddata,a,n):=APPEND(VECTOR(POLY(data SUB i SUB 1,a,n)=data SUB i SUB 2,i,1,DIMENSION(data),1),VECTOR(DPOLY(ddata SUB i SUB 1,a,n)=ddata SUB i SUB 2,i,1,DIMENSION(ddata),1))
UNK(n):=RHS(VECTOR(SOLVE(upsilon=upsilon,upsilon),i,1,n,1)` SUB 1)
ANS(x,data,ddata,a,n):=IF(DIMENSION(ans_:=SOLVE(EQNS(data,ddata,a,n),[a]))=0,[],POLY(x,(RHS(ans_)) SUB 1,n))
CURVEFIT1(x,data):=ANS(x,data,[],UNK(DIMENSION(data)),DIMENSION(data)-1)
CURVEFIT2(x,data,ddata):=ANS(x,data,ddata,UNK(DIMENSION(data)+DIMENSION(ddata)),DIMENSION(data)+DIMENSION(ddata)-1)
CURVEFIT(x,data,ddata):=IF(DIMENSION(ddata)>0,CURVEFIT2(x,data,ddata),CURVEFIT1(x,data),CURVEFIT1(x,data))
SPLINE_AUX(x,data,m):=SUM(CURVEFIT(x,[data SUB k,data SUB (k+1)],[[data SUB k SUB 1,m SUB k SUB 2]])*CHI(data SUB k SUB 1,x,data SUB (k+1) SUB 1),k,1,DIMENSION(data)-1)
SLOPE(data,m1):=ITERATES([v SUB 1+1,2*(data SUB (v SUB 1+1) SUB 2-data SUB (v SUB 1) SUB 2)/(data SUB (v SUB 1+1) SUB 1-data SUB (v SUB 1) SUB 1)-v SUB 2],v,[1,m1],DIMENSION(data)-1)
SPLINE(x,data,m1):=SPLINE_AUX(x,data,SLOPE(data,m1))
NEWT_ITERATES(u,x,x0,k):=ITERATES(x-u/DIF(u,x),x,x0,k)
NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=IF(k>0,NEWT_ITERATES(u,x,x0,k),?,SUBST(x-u/DIF(u,x),x,x0))
DRAW_NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=VECTOR([[v,0],[v,SUBST(u,x,v)],[NEWT(u,x,v),0]],v,ITERATES(NEWT(u,x,w),w,x0,k))
DRAW_COMPLEX(v):=VECTOR([RE(z),IM(z)],z,v)
BIS_AUX(u,x,a,b):=IF(SUBST(u,x,a)*SUBST(u,x,(a+b)/2)<0,[a,(a+b)/2],[(a+b)/2,b])
BISECT(u,x,v0,k):=IF(k>0,ITERATES(BIS_AUX(u,x,v SUB 1,v SUB 2),v,v0,k),?,BIS_AUX(u,x,v0 SUB 1,v0 SUB 2))
LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1)
MID(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+(k+0.5)*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1)
RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n)
TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/(2*n)*(SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(u,x,b)+2*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n-1))
SIMP(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/(6*n)*(SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(u,x,b)+2*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n-1)+4*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+(k+1/2)*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1))
D_BOX(x1,y1,x2,y2):=[[x1,y1],[x2,y1],[x2,y2],[x1,y2],[x1,y1]]
D_TRAP(x1,y1,x2,y2,x3,y3,x4,y4):=[[x1,y1],[x2,y2],[x3,y3],[x4,y4],[x1,y1]]
DRAW_LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_BOX(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DRAW_RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_BOX(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t+(b-a)/n)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DRAW_TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_TRAP(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t+(b-a)/n),t,SUBST(u,x,t)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DE(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=y=(y0+INT(q*#e^INT(p,x,x0,x),x,x0,x))/#e^INT(p,x,x0,x)
SEG(rc,x,y,δx,δy):=IF(ABS(rc)>1 AND δy<ABS(rc)*δx,[[x-δy/rc,y-δy],[x+δy/rc,y+δy]],[[x-δx,y-rc*δx],[x+δx,y+rc*δx]])
DF(r,x,x0,xm,m,y,y0,yn,n):=VECTOR(VECTOR(SEG(LIM(r,[x,y],[x0+j*(xm-x0)/m,y0+k*(yn-y0)/n]),x0+j*(xm-x0)/m,y0+k*(yn-y0)/n,(xm-x0)/(4*m),(yn-y0)/(4*n)),j,0,m),k,0,n)
EULER(r,x,y,x0,y0,xn,n):=ITERATES(v+(xn-x0)/n*[1,LIM(r,[x,y],v)],v,[x0,y0],n)

## hawaii-coursework/add-util.mth
SECANT(u,x,a,h):=y=(SUBST(u,x,a+h)-SUBST(u,x,a))/h*(x-a)+SUBST(u,x,a)
TANGENT(u,x,a):=y=SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(DIF(u,x),x,a)*(x-a)
POLY(x,a,n):=SUM(a SUB (i+1)*x^i,i,0,n)
DPOLY(x,a,n):=SUM(i*a SUB (i+1)*x^(i-1),i,1,n)
EQNS(data,ddata,a,n):=APPEND(VECTOR(POLY(data SUB i SUB 1,a,n)=data SUB i SUB 2,i,1,DIM(data),1),VECTOR(DPOLY(ddata SUB i SUB 1,a,n)=ddata SUB i SUB 2,i,1,DIM(ddata),1))
UNK(n):=VECTOR((SOLUTIONS(upsilon=upsilon,upsilon)) SUB 1,i,1,n,1)
ANS(x,data,ddata,a,n,v):=PROG(v:=SOLUTIONS(EQNS(data,ddata,a,n),a),IF(DIM(v)=0,[],POLY(x,v SUB 1,n)))
CURVEFIT1(x,data):=ANS(x,data,[],UNK(DIM(data)),DIM(data)-1)
CURVEFIT2(x,data,ddata):=ANS(x,data,ddata,UNK(DIM(data)+DIM(ddata)),DIM(data)+DIM(ddata)-1)
CURVEFIT(x,data,ddata):=IF(DIM(ddata)>0,CURVEFIT2(x,data,ddata),CURVEFIT1(x,data),CURVEFIT1(x,data))
SPLINE_AUX(x,data,m):=SUM(CURVEFIT(x,[data SUB k,data SUB (k+1)],[[data SUB k SUB 1,m SUB k SUB 2]])*CHI(data SUB k SUB 1,x,data SUB (k+1) SUB 1),k,1,DIMENSION(data)-1)
SLOPE1(data,m1):=ITERATES([v SUB 1+1,2*(data SUB (v SUB 1+1) SUB 2-data SUB (v SUB 1) SUB 2)/(data SUB (v SUB 1+1) SUB 1-data SUB (v SUB 1) SUB 1)-v SUB 2],v,[1,m1],DIM(data)-1)
SPLINE(x,data,m1):=SPLINE_AUX(x,data,SLOPE1(data,m1))
NEWT_ITERATES(u,x,x0,k):=ITERATES(x-u/DIF(u,x),x,x0,k)
NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=IF(k>0,NEWT_ITERATES(u,x,x0,k),?,SUBST(x-u/DIF(u,x),x,x0))
DRAW_NEWT(u,x,x0,k):=VECTOR([[v,0],[v,SUBST(u,x,v)],[NEWT(u,x,v),0]],v,ITERATES(NEWT(u,x,w),w,x0,k))
DRAW_COMPLEX(v):=VECTOR([RE(z),IM(z)],z,v)
BIS_AUX(u,x,a,b):=IF(SUBST(u,x,a)*SUBST(u,x,(a+b)/2)<0,[a,(a+b)/2],[(a+b)/2,b])
BISECT(u,x,v0,k):=IF(k>0,ITERATES(BIS_AUX(u,x,v SUB 1,v SUB 2),v,v0,k),?,BIS_AUX(u,x,v0 SUB 1,v0 SUB 2))
LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1)
MID(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+(k+0.5)*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1)
RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/n*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n)
TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/(2*n)*(SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(u,x,b)+2*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n-1))
SIMP(u,x,n,a,b):=(b-a)/(6*n)*(SUBST(u,x,a)+SUBST(u,x,b)+2*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+k*(b-a)/n),k,1,n-1)+4*SUM(SUBST(u,x,a+(k+1/2)*(b-a)/n),k,0,n-1))
D_BOX(x1,y1,x2,y2):=[[x1,y1],[x2,y1],[x2,y2],[x1,y2],[x1,y1]]
D_TRAP(x1,y1,x2,y2,x3,y3,x4,y4):=[[x1,y1],[x2,y2],[x3,y3],[x4,y4],[x1,y1]]
DRAW_LEFT(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_BOX(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DRAW_RIGHT(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_BOX(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t+(b-a)/n)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DRAW_TRAP(u,x,n,a,b):=VECTOR(D_TRAP(t,0,t+(b-a)/n,0,t+(b-a)/n,SUBST(u,x,t+(b-a)/n),t,SUBST(u,x,t)),t,a,b-(b-a)/n,(b-a)/n)
DE(p,q,x,y,x0,y0):=y=(y0+INT(q*#e^INT(p,x,x0,x),x,x0,x))/#e^INT(p,x,x0,x)
SEG(rc,x,y,δx,δy):=IF(ABS(rc)>1 AND δy<ABS(rc)*δx,[[x-δy/rc,y-δy],[x+δy/rc,y+δy]],[[x-δx,y-rc*δx],[x+δx,y+rc*δx]])
DF(r,x,x0,xm,m,y,y0,yn,n):=VECTOR(VECTOR(SEG(LIM(r,[x,y],[x0+j*(xm-x0)/m,y0+k*(yn-y0)/n]),x0+j*(xm-x0)/m,y0+k*(yn-y0)/n,(xm-x0)/(4*m),(yn-y0)/(4*n)),j,0,m),k,0,n)
EULER(r,x,y,x0,y0,xn,n):=ITERATES(v+(xn-x0)/n*[1,LIM(r,[x,y],v)],v,[x0,y0],n)
DE2(b,c,x,x0,y0,v0):=DSOLVE2_IV(b,c,0,x,x0,y0,v0)
EXTRACT_2_ELEMENTS(v,p,q):=[v SUB p,v SUB q]
EXTRACT_2_COLUMNS(a,c1,c2):=EXTRACT_2_ELEMENTS(a`,c1,c2)`
RK_AUX3(p,v,u_,c1,c2,c3):=(c1+LIM(p,v,u_+c3)+2*(c2+c3))/6
RK_AUX2(p,v,u_,c1,c2):=RK_AUX3(p,v,u_,c1,c2,LIM(p,v,u_+c2/2))
RK_AUX1(p,v,u_,c1):=RK_AUX2(p,v,u_,c1,LIM(p,v,u_+c1/2))
RK_AUX0(p,v,v0,n):=ITERATES(u_+RK_AUX1(p,v,u_,LIM(p,v,u_)),u_,v0,n)
RK(r,v,v0,h,n):=RK_AUX0(h*APPEND([1],r),v,v0,n)
